Complete BlockLayering production owner now has a genuine native16-state property/codec, normal native state/world callbacks, geometry, support, drop and replacement boundaries for foam_layer, sand_boron_layer, leaves_layer and oil_spill. All16 metadata identities survive; shape/quantity mask low3 bits while non-exempt replacement compares unmasked metadata. Selection and collision intentionally differ by one layer; metadata0 retains nonnull zero-height collision. No normal drops: native Items.AIR replaces old null sentinel. Ice/packed ice exclusion, same-owner full-layer support, special debris support, leaves-material support at every height, disabled melting and non-solid attachment faces remain.3,247 genuine native assertions and8 runtime mutants cover World/Chunk state roundtrips, state IDs, collision lists, all4 registrations, support/neighbor removal and native harvest superclass/order.

Layering probe compiles the complete production owner and existing WorldBlockState/WorldAirRemoval against actual Forge. NTM ModBlocks four field references and two special support classes are minimal test collaborators, not complete production registry/debris owners. World fixture retains real Chunk/state/removal with lighting disabled. Harvest proof preserves the mod's super-then-remove order and executes native stat/exhaustion framework calls; it does NOT certify original/native vanilla exhaustion amounts or universal inherited engine parity.
